6
Conclusion and discussion

We have proposed an amenably penalized noise corrected Poisson model
to study the relationship between the cognitive score and high
dimensional noisy neuroimage data. Under the sparsity assumption, we
established the parameter convergence rates in both
l_{1}
and
l_{2}

norms, the variable selection consistency property and the asymptotic
normality of a subvector with possibly infinitely many
components. Inference
tools are subsequently developed. The neuroimage application shows that the
inference tools generate scientifically meaningful results, which have
potential to be used to study the cognitive function and cognitive changes for
neurodegenerative diseases. Further research along this line is ongoing
in our group. The
neuroimage dataset
and computational code are available at
Jiang et al. 2021
.

Thanks to an anonymous referee, we would like to point out one
important extension. Instead of a constant matrix
\Omega
, we can
further allow
\Omega
to depend on both the covariate
{\bf X}
and the
response
Y
, hence
\Omega(Y,{\bf X})
, and assume
E({\bf U}|Y,{\bf X})={\bf 0}
. This would include heteroscedastic
measurement error and to allow dependent relation between
{\bf W}
and
Y

given
{\bf X}
. All the estimation and inference results will still hold
and the regularity conditions and proofs in the Suppement also
do not need to be further modified to accomodate this extension.

Establishing similar results in generalized linear models beyond
Poisson or general regression models with non-Gaussian
noise turns out to be surprisingly
difficult due to various technical obstacles.
The main difficult lies in being unable to construct a
loss function that is positive-definite at the true parameter
value. In the case when an estimating equation is available,
although one may be tempted to treat the
l_{2}
norm square of the
estimating equation as a loss function, we find other technical
issues arise partially because the Hessian of the loss function may
involve the response, hence some of the techniques used here cannot
be directly applied.
Likewise, extending the Poisson model to allow
overdispersion also turns out challenging, regardless if we use a
negative binomial model, or incorporate random effects, or use extra
observed covariates. All these will lead to models different from Poisson.
The biggest hurdle of
considering general regression model and/or non-Gaussian noise is to rigorously establish that the loss
function is locally convex. More investigation and
dedicated effort are needed in this aspect.

The assumption that the covariance of the measurement is known is
widely adopted
in the low and dimensional noisy data literature

(
Stefanski 1989
;
Cook & Stefanski 1995
;
Loh & Wainwright 2012
;
Sørensen et al. 2015
)
, because the
parameter estimation in the
noisy model with unknown noise
covariance is a challenging, especially in high dimensional
setting where the covariance is a high dimensional unknown parameter
to be estimated. Thresholding techniques as those proposed in

Bickel & Levina 2008
;
Cai & Liu 2011
;
Fan et al. 2011

can be used for the covariance estimation, but the theoretical
properties of the resulting estimators are involved, requiring careful
treatment of the additional error from the covariance
estimator. In a relatively simple situation when the error
variance can be estimated through estimating a parameter
{\bm{\gamma}}
via
solving
{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})={\bf 0}
, then writing
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})
as
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta},{\bm{\gamma}})
,
we can accomodate the additional parameter by concatenating
\bm{\beta}

with
{\bm{\gamma}}
and carrying out the subsequent analysis.
For example, in this case the result in Theorem

4
will be updated to

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}\cup S}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\{\begin{array}[]{cc}{\bf Q}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t},{\bm{\gamma}})&\partial^{2}{\cal L}(\bm{\beta},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\\
\partial{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}&\partial{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\end{array}\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\\
{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})\end{array}\right]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

Letting
{\bf M}\equiv{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})-\{\partial^{2}{\cal L}(\bm{\beta}_{t},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\}\{\partial{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\}^{-1}\{\partial{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}\}
, then this
leads to

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf M}^{-1}\left[\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+\{\partial^{2}{\cal L}(\bm{\beta}_{t},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\}\{\partial{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\}^{-1}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times{\bf f}_{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\gamma}})\right]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

We further conduct simulations to evaluate the proposed
adjustment in Section

B.6
of the supplementary document. The results
suggest that the proposed adjustment controls type I error rate when

\bm{\Omega}
contain small number of unknown parameters. Estimation and
testing when
\bm{\Omega}
has a large number of unknown parameters
are challenging problems and deserve much more extensive
investigation.

Supplementary Materials to “On High dimensional Poisson models with measurement error:
hypothesis testing for nonlinear nonconvex optimization”

Appendix A

We define an auxiliary function
q_{\lambda}(t)=\lambda|t|-\rho_{\lambda}(t)
to facilitate the theoretical derivation, where
q_{\lambda}(t)-\mu/2t^{2}
is concave and everywhere
differentiable as shown in Lemma
B.4
in the
supplementary material.

A.1
Conditions for the estimation consistency

Define
\alpha_{\min}({\bf M})
and
\alpha_{\max}({\bf M})
as the minimal and
maximal eigenvalues of the matrix
{\bf M}
, respectively. Further, we
define the sub-exponential norm and sub-Gaussian norm as

\displaystyle\|X\|_{\psi_{1}}=\sup_{k\geq 1}1/{k}E(|X|^{k})^{1/k},

and

\displaystyle\|X\|_{\psi_{2}}=\sup_{k\geq 1}1/\sqrt{k}E(|X|^{k})^{1/k}.

For notational convenience, let

\displaystyle A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}):=\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2),

\displaystyle g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w}):={\bf v}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf w},

and

\displaystyle g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w}):={\bf v}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}.

Definition 1
.

Loh & Wainwright 2012

(Lower-RE condition). The matrix
\bm{\Gamma}
satisfies a lower
restricted eigenvalue condition with curvature
\alpha_{1}>0
and
tolerance
\tau(n,p)>0
if

\displaystyle\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\beta}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau(n,p)\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}^{2},\forall\bm{\beta}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}.

Definition 2
.

Loh & Wainwright 2012

(Upper-RE condition). The matrix
\bm{\Gamma}
satisfies a upper
restricted eigenvalue condition with smoothness
a_{2}>0
and
tolerance
\tau(n,p)>0
if

\displaystyle\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\beta}\leq a_{2}\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\tau(n,p)\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}^{2},\forall\bm{\beta}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}.

We first state the regularity conditions as follows:

(C1)

(a)
\sup_{i=1,\ldots,n,\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1}|{\bf W}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf v}|\leq M_{W}\sqrt{\|{\bf v}\|_{0}}
for a positive constant
M_{W}
.
\|\bm{\Omega}\|_{2}=O(1)
.

(b)

\displaystyle D_{1}\leq\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}){\bf X}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\}]\leq\alpha_{\max}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}){\bf X}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\}]\leq D_{2},

\displaystyle D_{W1}\leq\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{\max}[E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}]\leq D_{W2},

\displaystyle\alpha_{\max}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)({\bf W}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}]\leq D_{W3},

and
E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}=O(1)
for any
\bm{\beta}
with
\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq 2R_{2}
.

(c)
E(\|{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2})\leq D_{\Omega}
,
for any
\bm{\beta}
with
\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq 2R_{2}
. Here
D_{1},D_{2},D_{W1},D_{W2},D_{W3},D_{\Omega}
are positive
constants.

(d)
\|{\bf C}\|_{2}=O(1)
and
\|({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}\|_{2}=O(1)
.

(e) The
L_{2}
norm of the true parameter
\bm{\beta}
is bounded, that is

\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq b_{0}
for some
0<b_{0}<\infty
.

(C2)

For
j=1,\ldots,p
, define

\displaystyle K_{j}:=\|U_{ij}\|_{\psi_{2}}=(2\Omega_{jj})^{1/2}\sup_{k\geq 1}k^{-1/2}\frac{\Gamma^{1/k}\{(k+1)/2\}}{\pi^{1/(2k)}},

where
\Gamma
is the Gamma function,
then there exist constants
m_{0},M_{0}
so that

\displaystyle m_{0}<K_{j}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}^{2}/n<M_{0},

uniformly for all
j
almost surely.

(C3)

Define

K_{Y}({\bf X}_{i})=\sup_{k\geq 1}k^{-1}E[|Y_{i}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})|^{k}|{\bf X}_{i}]^{1/k}.

There exist constants
m_{1},m_{2},M_{1},M_{2}
so that

\displaystyle m_{1}<\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{ij}^{2}K_{Y}({\bf X}_{i})^{2}/n<M_{1},

\displaystyle\max_{i}|X_{ij}|K_{Y}({\bf X}_{i})/\sqrt{\hbox{log}{n}}<M_{2},

uniformly for all
j=1,\ldots,p
almost surely.

(C4)

Sample size
n
and dimension
of covariates
p
satisfy

\displaystyle\hbox{log}(n)\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\leq C

for an absolute constant
C
.

(C5)

For
{\bf e}_{j}
,
j=1,\ldots,p
, define

\displaystyle K_{wij}(\bm{\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{k\geq 1}k^{-1/2}E[|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\rm T}{\bf e}_{j}-E\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\rm T}{\bf e}_{j}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}\}|^{k}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]^{1/k},

we assume
E\{K_{wij}(\bm{\beta}_{t})^{4}\}<Q_{0}
.
Then there exist constants
m_{3},M_{3},Q_{1}
so that

(i)

\displaystyle m_{3}<\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{wij}(\bm{\beta}_{t})^{2}\exp(2\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})/n<M_{3}

and

(ii)

\displaystyle|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})^{\rm T}{\bf e}_{j}|\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}\}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf e}_{j}}{\sqrt{n\hbox{log}(p)}}|<Q_{1}

uniformly for all
j=1,\dots,p
in probability.

(C6)

For vectors
{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
,
\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1,\|{\bf w}\|_{2}\leq 1
, for
\bm{\beta}
with

\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq 2R_{2}
,

K_{gvwi}(\bm{\beta}):=\sup_{k>1}1/kE(|[g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-E\{g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}\}]|^{k}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i})^{1/k}.

We assume
E\{K_{gvwi}(\bm{\beta})^{4}\}<Q_{01}
, and
E[\exp\{A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})K_{gvwi}^{2}(\bm{\beta})\}]<Q_{02}
.
We also assume that for all
{\bf v},{\bf w}
,

\displaystyle m_{4}<\sum_{i=1}^{n}|A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|^{2}K_{gvwi}(\bm{\beta})^{2}/n<M_{4},

(A.22)

\displaystyle m_{5}<\max_{i}|A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|K_{gvwi}(\bm{\beta})/\sqrt{\hbox{log}{n}}<M_{5},

(A.23)

and

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}|\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf v}^{\rm T}(A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})E[\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}){\bf w}|_{2}<Q_{2},

(A.24)

in probability.

(C7)

For vectors
{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
,
\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1,\|{\bf w}\|_{2}\leq 1
, for
\bm{\beta}
with

\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq 2R_{2}
,

K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta}):=\sup_{k>1}1/kE(|[g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-E\{g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}\}]|^{k}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i})^{1/k}.

We assume
E\{K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta})^{4}\}<Q_{11}
, and
E[\exp\{A^{4}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})K_{g_{1}vwi}^{2}(\bm{\beta})\}]<Q_{12}
.
We also assume that for all
{\bf v}
,

\displaystyle m_{6}<\sum_{i=1}^{n}|A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|^{2}K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta})^{2}/n<M_{6},

\displaystyle m_{7}<\max_{i}|A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta})/\sqrt{\hbox{log}{n}}<M_{7},

and

\displaystyle m_{61}<\sum_{i=1}^{n}|A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|^{2}K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta})^{2}/n<M_{61},

\displaystyle m_{71}<\max_{i}|A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})|K_{g_{1}vwi}(\bm{\beta})/\sqrt{\hbox{log}{n}}<M_{71},.

Further

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}|\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf v}^{\rm T}(A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})E[\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}){\bf w}|<Q_{3},

in probability and

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}|\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf v}^{\rm T}(A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})E[\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}){\bf w}|<Q_{31},

in probability.

A.2
Proof of Theorems in Section
4.2

A.2.1
Proof of Theorem
1

First denote
\widehat{{\bf v}}=\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}
, by the Taylor expansion of the first order derivative

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widehat{{\bf v}}=\widehat{\bf v}^{\rm T}\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}^{*})/\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bf v},

where
\bm{\beta}^{*}
is a point on the line connecting
\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}
and

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
and hence is in the feasible set. Therefore, by Lemma

B.6
we have

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widehat{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{1}^{2}.

(A.25)

We first show that
\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1
. If not, we have

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widehat{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}-2\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}R_{1}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{1}.

Together with (
12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\{-\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}{\bf A}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widehat{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}-2\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}R_{1}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{1}.

(A.26)

Further,

\displaystyle\{-\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}{\bf A}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widehat{{\bf v}}

(A.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{\|-\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}\|_{\infty}\|{\bf A}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\|_{\infty}\}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\lambda+\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\|_{\infty})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\lambda/2\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{1}.

The second inequality holds because the maximum row sum of
{\bf A}
is 1 and
\|\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda

by Condition (A1)–(A6) and Lemma
B.1
. The last equality holds because
\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda/2
by the
statement assumption.
Now combine (
A.27
) and (
A.26
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}^{-1}(2\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}R_{1}+3\lambda/2)\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}^{-1}(2\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}R_{1}+3\lambda/2)2R_{1}.

By the assumption that
\lambda\leq\alpha_{1}/(6R_{1})
and
n\geq\hbox{log}(p)(64\tau_{1}^{2}R_{1}^{4})/\alpha_{1}^{2}
in the statement, we conclude that
the right hand side is
at most one, which contradict to the hypothesis that
\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}>1
. Therefore
\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq 1
.

Further, since function
\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}
is convex function of
\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}
by Condition (A5), we
have

\displaystyle\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\mu/2\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\{\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}+\mu{\widehat{\bm{\beta}}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}\}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})

which implies

\displaystyle\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\{\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}\}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}\}{\bf A}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}).

Combine with (
12
), we have

\displaystyle\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}{\bf A}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\geq-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}),

and hence

\displaystyle\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|^{2}\geq\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}).

Now combine with (
A.25
), we have

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf v}}+\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|{\bf A}\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf v}}+\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|{\bf A}\|_{1}\|{\bf A}\|_{\infty}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf v}}+\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}.

This implies

\displaystyle(\alpha_{1}-\mu/2)\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

(A.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{1}^{2}+\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\|_{\infty}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{1}+\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{2R_{1}\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}+\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\|_{\infty}\right\}\{\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}\}+\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}).

Note that by the assumption that
n\geq\hbox{log}(p)(16R_{1}^{4}\tau_{1}^{4})/\alpha_{1}^{4}
, we have

\displaystyle 2R_{1}\tau_{1}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/2}=2R_{1}\tau_{1}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}\leq\alpha_{1}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}.

Further by the assumption that
4\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda
and
4\alpha_{1}\{\hbox{log}(p)/n\}^{1/4}\leq\lambda
in the lemma statement we obtain

\displaystyle 2R_{1}\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}+\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda/4+\lambda/4\leq\lambda/2.

Combine with (
A.28
) and Lemma
B.1
and
subadditivity of
\rho_{\lambda}
in Condition (A2), we have

\displaystyle(\alpha_{1}-\mu/2)\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

(A.29)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda/2\left\{\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\lambda}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}+\frac{\mu}{2\lambda}\|\widehat{\bf v}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{2}+\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}/2+\mu/4\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}.

Further, let
{\bf b}^{r}
be the vector containing the first
r
element of vector

{\bf b}
, and
{\bf b}^{-r}
the vector without the first
r

element.
By the condition that
{\bf C}\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}}={\bf t}
, we have

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{r}={\bf C}_{r}^{-1}({\bf t}-{\bf C}_{m-r}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r})
, and

\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{r}={\bf C}_{r}^{-1}({\bf t}-{\bf C}_{m-r}\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r})
.
We have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r}\|_{1}

(A.30)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}^{-r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}.

Now (
A.29
) becomes

\displaystyle 0\leq\left(\alpha_{1}-\frac{3\mu}{4}\right)\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}/2.

(A.31)

We consider two cases,
3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})>0
and
3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\leq 0
.
When
3\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\geq 0
,
by Lemma
B.2
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq 3\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\leq 3\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}-\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}.

(A.32)

Now from (
A.32
) we further have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}\leq 3\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}.

Substitue (
A.30
) and (
A.32
) into (
A.31
), we have

\displaystyle(2\alpha_{1}-\frac{3\mu}{2})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}-\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}+\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}+\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\lambda\sqrt{k}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{2}+\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{3\lambda\sqrt{k}+\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}.

Hence we have that when
3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})>0

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq\frac{6\lambda\sqrt{k}+2\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})}{4\alpha_{1}-3\mu}.

(A.33)

When
3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\leq 0
, by (
A.31
) and (
A.30
) we have

\displaystyle\left(\alpha_{1}-\frac{3\mu}{4}\right)\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lambda\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}/2

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}/2,

which implies that when
3/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-1/2\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})<0
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq\frac{2\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})}{4\alpha_{1}-3\mu}.

Together with (
A.33
), we always have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq\frac{6\lambda\sqrt{k}+2\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})}{4\alpha_{1}-3\mu}.

Further, the
L_{1}
distance is

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bf v}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\sqrt{k}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}+(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\sqrt{k}\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}+(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\|\widehat{{\bf v}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(4\sqrt{k}+\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})\frac{6\lambda\sqrt{k}+2\lambda(\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r})}{4\alpha_{1}-3\mu}.

This proves the result. ∎

A.2.2
Proof of Theorem
2

This proof is very similar to the proof of Theorem
1
and
is simpler, hence is omitted.

A.3
Proof of Theorems in Section
4.3

A.3.1
Supporting Theorem of Theorem
3
and its Proof

Theorem
A.1
.

Let
\alpha_{1}=\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]/2
.
Assume
\rho_{\lambda}
is
\mu
-amenable, for
\mu<\alpha_{1}
, and Conditions
(C1)
–
(C6)

hold. Define
{\bf A}=\{{\bf 0}_{(p-m)\times m},{\bf I}_{(p-m)\times(p-m)}\}
and

{\bf A}_{1}=\{{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times(p-m)}\}
.
Write
t_{1}\equiv\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r}
and

t\equiv(6\lambda\sqrt{k}+2\lambda t_{1})(4\alpha_{1}-3\mu)^{-1}.

Further we state
the following two conditions.

(a) The parameters
\lambda,R_{1},R_{2}
satisfy

\displaystyle 4\max\left\{\|\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\|_{\infty},\alpha_{1}(\hbox{log}(p)/n)^{1/4}\right\}\leq\lambda\leq\left[\alpha_{1}\left\{\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}16(4\sqrt{k}+t_{1})t/\lambda\right\}^{-1}\right]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\max\left\{\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}2\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{1},2(4\sqrt{k}+t_{1})t\right\}\leq R_{1}\leq\min\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{8\lambda},[n\alpha_{1}^{2}/\{64\tau_{1}^{2}\hbox{log}(p)\}]^{1/4},[n\alpha_{1}^{4}/\{16\tau_{1}^{4}\hbox{log}(p)\}]^{1/4}\right).

with

\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{1}\neq(4\sqrt{k}+t_{1})t

and

\max\left\{2\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2},2t\right\}\leq R_{2},

with

\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}\neq t.

(b)
Let
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
be the minimizer for
(
14
),
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}=(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T},{\bf 0}_{p-m-k}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
,
\widehat{{\bf z}}
and
\bm{\mu}_{4}
satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}=\bf 0.

(A.34)

There exist
\delta\in[4R_{1}\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}/\lambda,1]
so that

\|({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-\delta
, where
we name
({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})
as extended sub-gradient. In addition,

\displaystyle n\geq\max[\hbox{log}(p)\tau_{1}^{2}(m+k)^{2}/(\alpha_{1}-\mu)^{2},4\hbox{log}(p)\tau_{1}^{2}\{t_{1}+(2/\delta+1/2)\sqrt{k}\}^{4}/(\alpha_{1}-\mu)^{2}],~~\alpha_{1}>\mu

and

\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}
is
k
sparse.

Under the above conditions and the conditions (a) and (b),
(
7
) has a unique local
minimizer
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
.

Proof: We follow the primal-dual witness
construction introduced in
Wainwright 2009
.

Step i:

Following Lemma
B.10
in the supplementary material,
let

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
be the minimizer
for (
14
).
We can easily check that when replacing
\bm{\beta}
by
\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
,
{\bf X}
by

{\bf X}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
, and
p
by
m+k
, all the conditions of Theorem
1
are still
satisfied. Here to check the condition regarding
\alpha_{1}
, we note that for any
\bm{\beta}
,

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}}\inf_{\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,{\bf v}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf v}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}}\inf_{\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,{\bf v}\in\mathbb{R}^{m+k}}({\bf v}^{\rm T},{\bf 0}^{\rm T})E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}({\bf v}^{\rm T},{\bf 0}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}{\bf X}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}){\bf X}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}{\bf X}_{i,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}\}].

Therefore, Theorem
1
applied to the
m+k
dimensional case
leads to

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{1}\leq(4\sqrt{k}+t_{1})t\leq R_{1}/2,

and

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S}}\|_{2}\leq t\leq R_{2}/2.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{1}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S}}\|_{1}+\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{1}<R_{1}

and

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{{\mathcal{M}\cup S}}\|_{2}+\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}<R_{2}.

Hence
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
must be in the interior of the feasible
region.

Step ii:
We show that
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is a local minimum
for (
7
) by verifying the conditions in Lemma

B.12
in the supplementary material. Because

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})+\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1},

we can write
f=\mathcal{L}
,
g=q_{\lambda}
, and
({\bf x}^{*},{\bf v}^{*},{\bf w}_{1}^{*},{\bf w}_{2}^{*},\mu_{1}^{*},\mu_{2}^{*},\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*})=(\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{{\bf z}},{\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}},\widehat{\bf z}_{1},0,0,\bm{\mu}_{4})
, where
{\bf z}_{1}\in\partial\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}
. Lemma
B.4
in the supplementary
material ensures the
concavity and differentiability of
g({\bf x})-\mu/2\|{\bf x}\|_{2}^{2}
. Further, since
\mu_{1}^{*}=\mu_{2}^{*}=0
, (
B.2
) and
(
B.3
) are satisfied. (
B.4
) is satisfied by our
construction in (
A.34
). Therefore, it remains to verify
(
B.5
). We first show that
G^{*}\subseteq{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
so that (
B.5
) only needs to be satisfies for the
vectors belong to
{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
. Suppose this does not hold, let
\bm{\nu}\in G^{*}
such that

\hbox{supp}(\bm{\nu})\not\subseteq\mathcal{M}\cup S
. This implies there is an index
j\in{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}
such that
\nu_{j}\neq 0
.

Now we define
{\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime}
such that
({\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime})_{k}=({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{k}
for
k\neq j
, and
({\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime})_{j}={\rm sign}(\nu_{j})
, where
a_{k}
is the

k
th element in vector
{\bf a}
. Clearly
{{\bf z}}^{\prime}\in\partial\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
and

\displaystyle\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime}>\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}}

because
\|({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\|_{\infty}<1
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}\right]+\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime}+\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}\right]+\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}}+\bm{\nu}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}=0.

Now because
\bm{\nu}\in G^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}=0.

This implies

\displaystyle\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}\right]+\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{{\bf z}}^{\prime}>0

which contradicts with the requirement of
G^{*}
that

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}}\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}\right]+\lambda\bm{\nu}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}={0}.

Therefore,
G^{*}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
. Now by
construction
\hbox{supp}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\subset\mathcal{M}\cup S
, using Lemma

B.10
in the supplementary
material, we conclude that (
B.5
) holds with
\kappa=\mu
. Hence, all conditions of Lemma
B.12
in the supplementary
material are
satisfied, so we conclude

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is an isolated local minimum of
(
7
).

Now by Lemma
B.13
, because
\hbox{supp}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\subseteq\mathcal{M}\cup S
and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is an interior minimizer, the support of any stationary point of
(
7
) is a subset of
\mathcal{M}\cup S
. Hence, we can write any stationary point in the form of
\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}=\{\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T},{\bf 0}_{p-m-k}^{\rm T}\}^{\rm T}
, where
\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
is a
stationary point for (
14
).
Further, note that
(
14
) is strictly convex by Lemma

B.10
, and hence
\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
is unique in
the feasible set and
therefore
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
and

\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
are unique. Hence
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}=\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
is the unique
local minimum. This proves the result. ∎

A.3.2
Supporting Theorem of Theorem
4
and its Proof

Theorem
A.2
.

Let
\alpha_{1}=\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]/2
.
Assume
\rho_{\lambda}
is
\mu
-amenable, for

\displaystyle\max\left\{\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}2\|\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{1},2(4\sqrt{k}+\sqrt{m})\lambda\frac{6\sqrt{k}+2\sqrt{m}}{4\alpha_{1}-3\mu}\right\}\leq R_{1}

with

(A.35)

There exist

\displaystyle n

A.3.4
Proof of Theorem
3

First of all,
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}=\bf 0
by construction.
For any unit vector, recall that

\displaystyle{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}{\bf w}=\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf w}

Denoting
\nabla^{3}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})
to be the third order
gradient of
\mathcal{L}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf v}^{\rm T}\nabla^{3}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}){\bf w}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf w}\{{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}\}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}.

Hence define vectors
{\bf v},{\bf w}
such that their
j
th element
|v_{j}|>0,|w_{j}|>0
for
j\in\mathcal{M}\cup S
, and
|v_{j}|=|w_{j}|=0
for
j\notin\mathcal{M}\cup S
, and
\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=\|{\bf w}\|_{2}=1
. Firstly by Theorem
1
and
the condition that
\|{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}=O\{\sqrt{\max(m+k-r,r)/n}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}+\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\lambda\max(\sqrt{r},\sqrt{m-r},\sqrt{k})+\sqrt{(m+k)/n}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{2}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}\sqrt{m+k}

for some constants
C_{1},C_{2}>0
. Further recall that
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
.

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}^{\rm T}\left\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf w}\bigg|

(A.42)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\left[\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf w}\{{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}\}^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})

\displaystyle-{\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\}\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|\frac{n^{-1}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\frac{({\bf v}+{\bf w})^{\rm T}}{\sqrt{2}}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\frac{{\bf v}+{\bf w}}{\sqrt{2}}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|\frac{n^{-1}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\frac{({\bf v}-{\bf w})^{\rm T}}{\sqrt{2}}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\frac{{\bf v}-{\bf w}}{\sqrt{2}}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|\frac{({\bf v}+{\bf w})^{\rm T}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{\Omega}\frac{{\bf v}+{\bf w}}{\sqrt{2}}\frac{n^{-1}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|\frac{({\bf v}-{\bf w})^{\rm T}}{\sqrt{2}}\bm{\Omega}\frac{{\bf v}-{\bf w}}{\sqrt{2}}\frac{n^{-1}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf v}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}{\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf v}\bigg\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})\bigg\|_{2}\|(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}

(A.43)

\displaystyle+2\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})\{{\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\}\bigg|.

(A.44)

Now for (
A.42
), because by Condition

(C1)
in the
supplementary material, we have

\displaystyle|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})/\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}|\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M_{W}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}+\|\bm{\Omega}\|_{2}\|\bm{\beta}^{*}\|_{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2M_{W}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}.

The last equality holds because
\|\bm{\Omega}\|_{2}=O(1)
,
\|\bm{\beta}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\|\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}+\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}=O_{p}(1)+O_{p}\left\{{\hbox{log}(p)}/{n}\right\}^{1/4}\sqrt{m+k}=o_{p}(\sqrt{m+k})
.
From Corollary

B.4
in the supplementary
material, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta})|

\displaystyle\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}-E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta})|\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2M_{W}\sqrt{(m+k)/n}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\|_{2}

with probability
1-O[\exp\{-(m+k)\}]
.
Further since
\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\}=O(1)
due to Condition C1(b), hence
we get

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\left\{n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\right\}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{2M_{W}\sqrt{(m+k)/n}+O_{p}(1)\}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\|_{2}).

For (
A.43
), we first have

\displaystyle\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\{\sqrt{(m+k)/n}\}

by Corollary
B.3
in the supplementary
material.
Further
\|E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\}\|_{2}=O(1)
.
Hence (
A.43
) is of order
O_{p}(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\|_{2})
.
Now for (
A.44
), because

\displaystyle\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v},

by Corollary
B.3
in the supplementary
material, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\}=O_{p}(\sqrt{(m+k)/n})

with probability of the order
1-O\{\exp(m+k)\}
. Further because
E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\}=O(1)
, the third term of the last line
is of the order
O_{p}(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}^{\rm T}\left\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf w}\bigg|=O_{p}(\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})

Hence for some positive constants
C_{3}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}^{\rm T}\left\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf w}\bigg|\leq C_{3}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)

(A.45)

with probability
1-O[\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\}]
.
Further, by Corollary
B.2
in the supplementary
material, we also have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right]{\bf w}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right]_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}{\bf w}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg|

(A.46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\{\sqrt{(m+k)/n}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right]

with probability
1-2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\}
.
Further, recall that

{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
.

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\left\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf w}\bigg|

(A.47)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\left[\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf w}\{{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}\}^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})

\displaystyle-{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\}\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}

\displaystyle+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}

\displaystyle+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg|\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}\bigg|\bigg|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}

\displaystyle+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\sup_{i}\bigg|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}\sup_{i}\bigg|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

(A.48)

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bf w}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

(A.49)

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf w}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}

\displaystyle+{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|.

(A.50)

For (
A.47
), from Corollary

B.4
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})|

\displaystyle\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}-E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})|\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2M_{W}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

with probability
1-O[\exp\{-\hbox{log}(p)\}]
.
Further since
\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\}=O(1)
due to Condition C1(b), hence
we get

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}\left\{n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\right\}\sup_{i}|({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{2M_{W}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}+O_{p}(1)\}\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\|_{2}).

For (
A.48
), we use the same argument as those lead to the
order of (
A.42
), we have (
A.48
) is of order
O_{p}(\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})
.
For (
A.49
), we use the same argument as those lead to the
order of (
A.43
), we have (
A.49
) is of order
O_{p}(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})
.

Now for (
A.50
), because

\displaystyle\bigg|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1},

by Corollary
B.3
in the supplementary
material, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}

\displaystyle-E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\}=O_{p}(\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n})

with probability of the order
1-O[\exp\{-\hbox{log}(p)\}]
. Further because
E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}){\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}_{1}\}=O(1)
, and because of the same
argument as those lead to (
A.49
), we conclude that
(
A.50
)
is of the order
O_{p}(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})
.
Hence follow (
A.42
), and by Conditions

(C1)
, Corollaries

B.2
–
B.5
in the supplementary
material, for
positive constants
C_{4}
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\left\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf w}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\sqrt{m+k}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|_{2})

(A.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{4}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right]{\bf w}\bigg|

(A.52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{1(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}^{\rm T}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right]_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}{\bf w}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}[\sqrt{\max\{(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)\}/n}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right],

with probabilities to the order of
1-2\exp[-\max\{(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)\}]
.

Combine (
A.45
) and (
A.46
) with Lemma

B.11
in the supplementary
material,

\displaystyle\|(\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S})(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{2}

(A.53)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\|\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}}{\{1-\|\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{2}\|\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\|\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-{\bf Q}_{0(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{9}\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right],

and hence

\displaystyle\|(\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S})(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

(A.54)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{9}(m+k)^{3/2}\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}.

Further,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

(A.55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|[\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})]\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}

\displaystyle-\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}_{1}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}\bigg|{\bf v}_{1(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}^{\rm T}[\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})-{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})]{\bf w}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg|

\displaystyle\times\|\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{2}\bigg\|\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}\bigg\|\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\bigg\|_{2}\bigg\|\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\{{\bf Q}_{0(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{10}\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right]\sqrt{m+k}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}

\displaystyle+C_{11}\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right]\sqrt{m+k}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}

(A.56)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}.

(
A.55
) is obtained by using (
A.51
) and
(
A.52
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-[{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})]^{-1}\|_{2}+\|\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O_{p}(1).

(
A.56
) is obtained by using (
A.45
),

) and the fact that

\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta})\|_{2}=O(1)
.
Therefore, now note that
n\geq c_{\infty}(m+k)^{4}\hbox{log}(p)
and

\lambda=O[\{\hbox{log}(p)/n\}^{1/4}]

by the statement
assumption, together with Condition

(C4)
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

(A.57)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}+o_{p}(\lambda)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c},\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta})\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\bigg\|_{2}\bigg\|\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{2}+o_{p}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{m+k}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}+o_{p}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(\lambda).

Therefore

\displaystyle\|({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\|_{\infty}

(A.58)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\lambda^{-1}\left[-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\right]+\lambda^{-1}\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\left[\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]+\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}\right)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lambda^{-1}\bigg\|\left[-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\right]\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{-1}\bigg\|\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{-1}\bigg\|\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left(\left[-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]+\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right)\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{-1}\|\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-1}\bigg\|\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left(\left[-\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]+\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right)\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{-1}\bigg\|\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left[\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\bigg\|_{\infty}+o(1).

Now recall (
A.41
)
and

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

(A.59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left[-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right].

Hence

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{\infty}

(A.60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\left[\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left[-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{p}(\lambda)+\lambda\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+\lambda\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(\lambda)+2\lambda c_{\infty}+2\lambda c_{\infty},

where
o_{p}(\lambda)
in the second inequality is obtained by using the same
argument as those lead to
(
A.57
). The other two terms are obtained by
using Lemma
B.4
such
that

\bigg\|\left[\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\bigg\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda.

The last equality holds because

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+\|\{{\bf Q}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{\infty}

by (
A.54
).
And similarly, we have

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}

\displaystyle-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\|\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2c_{\infty}.

Now because
\min(|\beta_{j}|)\geq\lambda(\gamma+5c_{\infty})
for
j\in S
, we have

\displaystyle\min|\widehat{\beta}_{j}|\geq\lambda\gamma

and
q_{\lambda}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta})_{j}=\lambda{\rm sign}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})=\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{j}
.
Hence by Lemma
B.3
in the supplementary
material, we have

\displaystyle\left\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\lambda({\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}=\bf 0.

(A.61)

Inserting (
A.61
) into (
A.58
), the first two terms of
(
A.58
) are zero hence

\|({\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf z})_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}\|=o_{p}(1)<1.

This implies that
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
has support in
\mathcal{M}\cup S
. Further, because of (
A.60
),

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is unique in the feasible set.
Therefore, using (
A.41
) together with (
A.61
) and (
A.59
),
we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}-\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}^{*})\}^{-1})

\displaystyle\times\left[-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta})\}^{-1}-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})

\displaystyle\times\left[\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}=\bf 0
.
Now because

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta})(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})+\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right\}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})+\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

The last equality holds because
