\displaystyle\|\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right\}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1),

by (
A.46
),

\displaystyle\|\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)

by (
A.45
), and
\|{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}=O(1)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})

\displaystyle\times\left[\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}+{\bf Q}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

Further, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-[({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}]^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})^{-1}\}-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})\left[\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})[({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}]^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}-[({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}]^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\{({{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}[\{({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}\}^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

∎

A.3.5
Proof of Theorem
4

The proof is very similar to that of Theorem
3
hence is omitted.

A.4
Proof of Results in Section
4.4

A.4.1
Proof of Lemma
1

First note that

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\bm{\omega}_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})

\displaystyle\times n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}.

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}\left(\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times n^{-1/2}\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\right)={\bf I}_{r\times r}.

(A.62)

Further,

\displaystyle r^{1/4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\|n^{-1/2}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})

(A.63)

\displaystyle\times\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle r^{1/4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\|n^{-1/2}\{Y_{i}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle r^{1/4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\|n^{-1/2}\{Y_{i}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle r^{1/4}D\sum_{i=1}^{n}n^{-3/2}(\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle+\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1),

where
D
is a positive constant. The second to the last inequality
holds by Condition
(D1)
. Also because

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}\|{\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}|{\bf W}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf v}|/\|{\bf v}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)M_{W}\sqrt{m+k}=O(\sqrt{m+k}),

where
{\bf v}
is a
p
dimensional vector with
\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=m+k
, the last
inequality holds by Condition
(C1)
(a). Further since
\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}=O(1)
,

\displaystyle r^{1/4}D\sum_{i=1}^{n}n^{-3/2}(\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf W}_{i\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle+\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{-1}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\|_{2}^{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\{r^{1/4}(m+k)^{3/2}n^{-1/2}\}=o_{p}(1),

by the Condition that
n\geq c_{\infty}(m+k)^{4}\hbox{log}(p)
.
Combine (
A.4.1
) and (
A.63
), and using Lemma

B.14
in Section
B.4.3
, let
{\bf Z}
is a
standard Gaussian random variable we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\sup_{\mathcal{C}}|\Pr(\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\bm{\omega}_{n}\in{\mathcal{C}})-\Pr({\bf Z}\in{\mathcal{C}})|=0.

(A.64)

Now we choose

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}_{x}}=\{{\bf z}:\|{\bf z}-\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}\leq x\},

then

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}|\Pr(\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\bm{\omega}_{n}\in{\mathcal{C}_{x}})-\Pr({\bf Z}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{x})|=0,

which implies

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}|\Pr(T_{0}\leq x)-\Pr\{\chi^{2}(r,n{\bf h}_{n}^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t}){\bf h}_{n})\leq x\}|=0,

where
\chi^{2}(r,\gamma)
is a non-central chi-square
distribution, with non-centrality parameter
\gamma
. ∎

A.4.2
Proof of Theorem
5

From Theorem
4
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

Hence,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}{\bf C}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}{\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\omega}_{n}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}.

Further, because
{\bf C}\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}}={\bf t}+{\bf h}_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})({\bf C}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}}-{\bf t})=\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})\bm{\omega}_{n}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}+\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}^{-1/2}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a}){\bf h}_{n}.

Further because
{\bf h}_{n}=O\{\sqrt{\max(m+k-r,r)/n}\}
,

\displaystyle n({\bf C}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}}-{\bf t})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})({\bf C}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a\mathcal{M}}-{\bf t})

(A.65)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle+(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\{\bm{\Psi}^{-1}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})-\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle+\|\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})-\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{0}+o_{p}(r).

The last equality holds because
T_{0}
converge in distribution to a
non-central chi-square distribution with degree freedom
r
as
shown in Lemma
1
and
hence
T_{0}
is of the order
O_{p}(r)
. Also because

\displaystyle\|\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})/\alpha_{\min}\{\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})/c_{\bm{\Psi}}

by Condition
(D2)
,
we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Psi}^{-1}(\widehat{\bf\Sigma},\widehat{\bf Q},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{a})-\bm{\Psi}^{-1}({\bf\Sigma},{\bf Q},\bm{\beta}_{t})\|_{2}\|\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\left\{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}\right\}^{1/4}(m+k)\right]O_{p}(T_{0}).

Therefore,
T_{W}-T_{0}=o_{p}(r)
.
∎

A.4.3
Proof of Theorem
6

From Theorem
3
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}-\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1})\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle+\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}[\{({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}\}^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}=\bf 0
.
By Taylor expansion we have

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}({\bm{\beta}_{t}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}({\bm{\beta}_{t}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}+{\bf Q}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}_{t})_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle+\{{\bf A}_{2}^{*}[\{({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}\}^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}]\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

where
\bm{\beta}^{*}
is a point in between
\bm{\beta}_{t}
and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
. The second
equality holds by (
A.45
) and
(
A.46
) in Appendix.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1/2}{\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}^{-1}{\bf A}_{2}^{*}\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}_{t})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1/2}{\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]\{{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\bm{\beta}_{t})\}^{-1}\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}({\bm{\beta}_{t}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\right\}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1/2}\bm{\omega}_{n}\{1+o_{p}(1)\},

The first
equality holds by (
A.45
) and
(
A.46
) in the supplementary material.
And similarly

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1/2}{\bf C}[{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times k}]\{\widehat{{\bf Q}}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S,\mathcal{M}\cup S}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}^{-1}\{{\bf A}_{2}^{*}[\{({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}{\bf C},{\bf 0}_{r\times k}\}^{\rm T}{\bf h}_{n}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1/2}{\bf h}_{n}\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

and hence

\displaystyle T_{S}=(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})^{\rm T}\bm{\Psi}(\widehat{{\bf\Sigma}},\widehat{{\bf Q}},\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{-1}(\bm{\omega}_{n}+\sqrt{n}{\bf h}_{n})\{1+o_{p}(1)\}

Now the same steps in (
A.65
) lead to
T_{S}-T_{0}=o_{p}(r)
.
∎

Appendix B

B.1
Some Lemmas on the Penalty Function

Lemma
B.1
.

Conditions (A1)–(A4)
imply that
\rho_{\lambda}
is
\lambda
-Lipschitz and all
sub gradients and derivatives of
\rho_{\lambda}
are bounded by

\lambda
in magnitude.
Conditions (A1)–(A5) imply

\displaystyle\lambda\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}\leq\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\mu/2\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2},\forall\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\in\mathbb{{\bf R}}^{p-m}

Proof: This lemma is a direct consequence of Lemma 4 in

Loh & Wainwright 2015
.

Lemma
B.2
.

Suppose
\rho_{\lambda}
satisfies Conditions (A1)–(A5). Let

{\bf v}\in\mathbb{R}^{p-m}
, let
\mathcal{A}
be the index set of
k

largest elements of
{\bf v}
in magnitude, and
let
\mathcal{A}^{c}
be the index set of the remaining
p-m-k

elements of
{\bf v}
.
Suppose
\xi>0
and satisfies

\displaystyle\xi\rho_{\lambda}({\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}})-\rho_{\lambda}({\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}^{c}})\geq 0.

Then

\displaystyle\xi\rho_{\lambda}({\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}})-\rho_{\lambda}({\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}^{c}})\leq\lambda(\xi\|{\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}-\|{\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}).

Moreover, if
\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}^{c}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p-m}
is
k
-sparse, then for a vector

\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p-m}
such that
\xi\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})>0
and
\xi\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\xi\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\leq\lambda(\xi\|{\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}}\|_{1}-\|{\bf v}_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}\|_{1}),

where
{\bf v}=\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\bm{\beta}_{t\mathcal{M}^{c}}
,
\mathcal{A}
is the index set of the
k

largest elements of
{\bf v}
in magnitude, and

\mathcal{A}^{c}
is the index set of the remaining
p-m-k
elements of
{\bf v}
.

Proof: This lemma is a direct consequence of Lemma 5 in

Loh & Wainwright 2015
. ∎

Lemma
B.3
.

Suppose
\rho_{\lambda}
is
(\mu,\gamma)
-amenable,

|\widehat{\beta}_{j}|\geq\lambda\gamma
for
j\in\mathcal{M}\cup S
, then

q_{\lambda}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})=\lambda{\rm sign}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})
.

Proof: Because
\rho_{\lambda}
is
(\mu,\gamma)
-amenable,

\rho^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})=0
by Condition (A6) and (A1). Hence
q_{\lambda}^{\prime}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})=\partial\lambda|\widehat{\beta}_{j}|/\partial\widehat{\beta}_{j}=\lambda{\rm sign}(\widehat{\beta}_{j})
. This proves the result. ∎

Lemma
B.4
.

Consider a
\mu
-amenable regularizer
\rho_{\lambda}
. Then

(a)
|\rho_{\lambda}^{\prime}(t)|\leq\lambda
for all
t\neq 0
.

(b) The function
q_{\lambda}(t)-\mu/2t^{2}
is concave and
everywhere differentiable, where
q_{\lambda}(t)=\lambda|t|-\rho_{\lambda}(t)
.

Proof: This lemma is a direct consequence of Lemma 5 in

Loh & Wainwright 2017
. ∎

B.2
Some Lemmas on the Criterion Function

Lemma
B.5
.

Assume Conditions
(C1)
–
(C4)

hold. There exists a constant
c_{1}>0
so that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left[n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp(\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}_{t})\}\|_{\infty}>c_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right]\leq 6p^{-1}.

Proof: The lemma is the direct consequence of Corollary 1 in

Jiang & Ma 2021
. We omit the proof here.

Lemma
B.6
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
,
(C4)
and

(C6)
hold, then
for any
\bm{\beta}
with
\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}
, and
for sufficiently large
n
and
p
,
with probability
1-O[\exp\{-\sqrt{n\hbox{log}p}\}]
,

n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}
satisfies the lower and upper-RE conditions
with

\alpha_{1}=\min_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}}\alpha_{\min}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]/2,

\alpha_{2}=\max_{\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|\bm{\beta}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}}3\alpha_{\max}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]/2

and

\tau(n,p)=\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}

for a bounded positive constant
\tau_{1}
.

Proof: The lemma is a direct consequence of Lemma 12 in

Jiang & Ma 2021
with
c=1
. ∎

Corollary
B.1
.

Assume Conditions
(C1)
–
(C4)
to hold,
\|{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}=O\{\sqrt{\max(m+k-r,r)/n}\}
and
m+k=o(n^{1/3})
, then there exists
a positive constant
c_{10}
so that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\|_{\infty}\leq c_{10}\max[\sqrt{(m+k)/n},\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}],

with probability
1-O(p^{-1})-O[\exp\{-\sqrt{n\hbox{log}(p)}\}]
.

Proof:

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\widecheck{\bm{\beta}})\}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{Y_{i}{\bf W}_{i}-\exp({\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-{\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}})\}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})\}-\exp({\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-{\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}})\}\|_{\infty}.

Let
\mathbb{K}_{0}=\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}:{\bf v}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}=0,\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1\}.
Then,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})-\exp({\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-{\bm{\beta}_{t}}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}{\bm{\beta}_{t}})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}\{\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}_{t}}\}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf v}^{\rm T}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}{\bf v}\|\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}_{t}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\alpha_{\max}[E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}]/2\|{\bf C}^{\rm T}({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}\|_{2}\|{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}+\tau_{1}m\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|{\bf C}^{\rm T}({\bf C}{\bf C}^{\rm T})^{-1}\|_{2}\|{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{10}\max[\sqrt{(m+k)/n},\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}]

for some constants
c_{10}
. The first inequality holds by the Taylor
expansion with
\bm{\beta}^{*}
be the point on the line connecting
\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}
and

\bm{\beta}
. The second inequality holds because
\widecheck{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}
only
has
m
nonzero elements supported in
\mathcal{M}
, and hence only the
corresponding
m\times m
sub-matrix in
n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp(\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{*\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*}/2)\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}
contributes to the
L_{2}

norm. The third inequality holds by Lemma
B.6
and the fact that

{\bf v}
only as
m
non-zero elements. The last equality holds because

\|{\bf h}_{n}\|_{2}=O\{\sqrt{\max(m+k-r,r)/n}\}\leq O(\sqrt{m+k/n})
, and

m\sqrt{m+k/n}\to 0
because
m+k=o(n^{1/3})
. This proves the
results. ∎

Lemma
B.7
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
and
(C6)
hold. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
, define
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{324e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{36eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)\right),

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{36e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{12eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)+\hbox{log}(p)\right),

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2\hbox{log}(p)\right).

Proof:
By Lemma 1 statement 3 and Lemma 3 statement 3 in
Jiang & Ma 2021
, we can see
that the square of a conditional sub-Gaussian
variable is sub-exponential. Now because
{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})
given
{\bf X}_{i}
and
\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}
is normal, and
hence

{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}

is conditional sub-exponential. Now by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, and
without loss of generality we assume
{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}\geq{\bf w}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}
, we
have

\displaystyle|{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}|\leq{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}.

Hence, by Lemma 3 statement 3 in
Jiang & Ma 2021
, we have for some
bounded positive
K_{3}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i})
,

\displaystyle E[\exp\{|{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}|/K_{3}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i})\}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle E[\exp\{|{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf v}|/K_{3}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i})\}|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}]\leq e

Hence,
{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}
is also conditional sub-exponential
variable. Therefore,
we have that

\displaystyle g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-E\{g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})|\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i},{\bf X}_{i}\}

is centered sub-exponential.
Then using the same argument as those that lead to Lemma 8
in
Jiang & Ma 2021
and Condition
(C6)
, for any unit
vectors
{\bf v},{\bf w}
,
we can show that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]\right).

Now we define

\mathcal{B}=\{{\bf u}_{1},\ldots,{\bf u}_{r}\}\subset{\mathbb{K}}
to be a 1/3-cover of
{\mathbb{K}}
, if for every
{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}
, there is some
{\bf u}_{i}\in\mathcal{B}
such that
\|{\bf v}-{\bf u}_{i}\|_{2}\leq 1/3
. Define

\Delta{\bf v}={\bf v}-{\bf u}_{j}
where
{\bf u}_{j}=\arg\min_{{\bf u}_{i}}\|{\bf v}-{\bf u}_{i}\|_{2}
. We have
\|\Delta{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1/3
. Similarly define
{\bf u}_{k}=\arg\min_{{\bf u}_{i}}\|{\bf w}-{\bf u}_{i}\|_{2}
for
{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}
.
By

ledoux2013
, we can construct
\mathcal{B}
with
|\mathcal{B}|<9^{(m+k)}
.
Now for
{\bf v}_{1},{\bf v}_{2}\in\mathbb{K}
, define

\displaystyle\Phi({\bf v}_{1},{\bf v}_{2})={\bf v}_{1}^{\rm T}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})\{({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}-\bm{\Omega}\}-E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}}{n}\right]{\bf v}_{2}.

We have

\displaystyle|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\Phi(\Delta{\bf v}+{\bf u}_{j},\Delta{\bf w}+{\bf u}_{k})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{j,k}|\Phi({\bf u}_{j},{\bf u}_{k})|+\max_{i}|\Phi(\Delta{\bf v},{\bf u}_{i})|+\max_{i}|\Phi({\bf u}_{i},\Delta{\bf w})|+|\Phi(\Delta{\bf v},\Delta{\bf w})|.

Since
\|3\Delta{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1
and
\hbox{supp}(3\Delta{\bf v})\subseteq\mathbb{K}
,

3\Delta{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}
.
It follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{j,k}|\Phi({\bf u}_{j},{\bf u}_{k})|+1/3\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}}\max_{i}|\Phi(3\Delta{\bf v},{\bf u}_{i})|+1/3\sup_{{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}\max_{i}|\Phi({\bf u}_{k},3\Delta{\bf w})|+1/9\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi(3\Delta{\bf v},3\Delta{\bf w})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{j,k}|\Phi({\bf u}_{j},{\bf u}_{k})|+2/3\{\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi(3\Delta{\bf v},3\Delta{\bf v})|\}^{1/2}\{\max_{i}|\Phi({\bf u}_{i},{\bf u}_{i})|\}^{1/2}+1/9\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{j,k}|\Phi({\bf u}_{j},{\bf u}_{k})|+\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}\{2/3|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|+1/9|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|\}.

Hence,

\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|\leq 9/2\max_{j,k}|\Phi({\bf u}_{j},{\bf u}_{k})|
.
By a union bound while considering
|\mathcal{B}|<9^{2(m+k)}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>9/2nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\max_{j,k}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\max_{j,k}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v}/\|{\bf v}\|_{2},{\bf w}/\|{\bf w}\|_{2})-{\bf v}/\|{\bf v}\|_{2}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}/\|{\bf w}\|_{2}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)\right).

It also follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},j}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf u}_{j})|+1/3\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},3\Delta{\bf w})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},j}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf u}_{j})|+1/3\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}\{|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|,

so

\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf w})|\leq 3/2\max_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},j}|\Phi({\bf v},{\bf u}_{j})|
.
Because
{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1}
is a vector with a single
nonzero entry
1
, there are only
p-m-k
elements in

\mathbb{K}_{1}
. We thus have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>3/2nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\max_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf u}_{j}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf u}_{j})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf u}_{j}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)+\hbox{log}(p)\right).

This proves the result.
Further, since
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}
contains only unit
vectors, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in\mathbb{K}_{1},{\bf w}\in\mathbb{K}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2\hbox{log}(p)\right).

∎

Corollary
B.2
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
,
(C4)
and
(C6)
hold and
m+k=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
, define
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
, there are
a_{0}>0,a_{1}>0,b_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>na_{0}\sqrt{\frac{m+k}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\},

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>na_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\max[m+k,\hbox{log}(p)]}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp[-\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}],

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nb_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp[-\hbox{log}(p)].

Proof: By Lemma
B.7
, take
t=a_{0}\sqrt{(m+k)/n}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{a_{0}^{2}(m+k)}{324e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{a_{0}\sqrt{n(m+k)}}{36eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\frac{a_{0}^{2}(m+k)}{324e^{2}M_{4}}+2(m+k)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\}

The second to the last equality holds because
m+k=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
. The last equality holds
by choosing
a_{0}=\sqrt{972e^{2}M_{4}}
.

Further, by the second relation in Lemma
B.7
,
take
t=a_{1}\sqrt{\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}/n}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{a_{1}^{2}\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}}{36e^{2}M_{4}},\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{a_{1}\sqrt{n\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}}}{12eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)+\hbox{log}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-\min\left(\frac{a_{1}^{2}\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}}{36e^{2}M_{4}},\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.a_{1}\max[\hbox{log}(p)\sqrt{n}/\{12eM_{5}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)}\hbox{log}(n)\},\sqrt{n(m+k)}/\{12eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)\}]\right)

\displaystyle\left.+2(m+k)+\hbox{log}(p)\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left(\frac{a_{1}^{2}\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}}{36e^{2}M_{4}},\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.a_{1}\max[\hbox{log}(p)/(12eM_{5}C),\sqrt{n(m+k)}/\{12eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)\}]\right)

\displaystyle\left.+2(m+k)+\hbox{log}(p)\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-\min\left[\frac{a_{1}^{2}\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}}{36e^{2}M_{4}},\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.a_{1}\max\{\hbox{log}(p)/(12eM_{5}C),(m+k)/(12eM_{5})\}\right]+3\max\{(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)\}\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-a_{2}\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}+3\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp[-\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}],

where
a_{2}=\min\{a_{1}^{2}/(36e^{2}M_{4}),a_{1}/(12eM_{5}C),a_{1}/(12eM_{5})\}
, and we select
a_{1}
such that
a_{2}\geq 4
.
The third equality holds because
\hbox{log}(n)\leq C\sqrt{n/\hbox{log}(p)}
by Condition
(C4)
. The fourth equality holds
(m+k)=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
.

In addition, by the third relation in Lemma
B.7
,
take
t=b_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{i}{\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{b_{1}^{2}\hbox{log}(p)}{16e^{2}M_{4}},\frac{b_{1}\sqrt{n\hbox{log}(p)}}{8eM_{5}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2\hbox{log}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-\min\left(\frac{b_{1}^{2}\hbox{log}(p)}{16e^{2}M_{4}},b_{1}\hbox{log}(p)\sqrt{n}/\{8eM_{5}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)}\hbox{log}(n)\}\right)+2\hbox{log}(p)\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left(\frac{b_{1}^{2}\hbox{log}(p)}{16e^{2}M_{4}},b_{1}\hbox{log}(p)/(8eM_{5}C)\right)+2\hbox{log}(p)\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-b_{12}\hbox{log}(p)+2\hbox{log}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp[-\hbox{log}(p)],

where
b_{12}=\min\{b_{1}^{2}/(16e^{2}M_{4}),b_{1}/(8eM_{5}C)\}
, and we select
b_{1}
such that
b_{12}\geq 3
.
The second inequality holds because
\hbox{log}(n)\leq C\sqrt{n/\hbox{log}(p)}
by Condition
(C4)
.
∎

Lemma
B.8
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
and
(C7)
hold. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
, define
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
. Then

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{324e^{2}M_{6}},\frac{nt}{36eM_{7}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)\right),

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{36e^{2}M_{6}},\frac{nt}{12eM_{7}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)+\hbox{log}(p)\right\},

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|>nt\vphantom{\int_{0}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-\min\left[\frac{nt^{2}}{16e^{2}M_{6}},\frac{nt}{8eM_{7}\hbox{log}(n)}\right]+2\hbox{log}(p)\right\}.

Proof: The lemma holds by using the same arguments as those
lead to Lemma
B.7
. ∎

Corollary
B.3
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
,
(C4)

(C7)

hold,
m+k=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
, define
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
. There are
a_{2}>0,a_{3}>0,b_{3}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|>na_{2}\sqrt{\frac{(m+k)}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\},

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|

\displaystyle\left.>na_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\max[(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)]}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left[-\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}\right].

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A^{2}(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(2\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|

\displaystyle\left.>nb_{3}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right)\leq 2\exp\{-\hbox{log}(p)\}.

Proof: The corollary follows the same arguments as those
lead to Corollary
B.2
.∎

Corollary
B.4
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
,
(C4)

(C7)

hold,
m+k=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
, define
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
. There are
a_{21}>0,a_{31}>0,b_{31}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|>na_{21}\sqrt{\frac{(m+k)}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\},

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|

\displaystyle\left.>na_{31}\sqrt{\frac{\max[(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)]}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left[-\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}\right],

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}[A(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i})g_{1}({\bf W}_{i},\bm{\beta},{\bf v},{\bf w})\right.

\displaystyle-{\bf v}^{\rm T}E\{\exp(\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta}/2)({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}\}{\bf w}]|

\displaystyle\left.>nb_{31}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right)\leq 2\exp\{-\hbox{log}(p)\}.

Proof: The corollary follows the same arguments as those that
lead to Corollary
B.3
.∎

Lemma
B.9
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
holds,
m+k=o\{n/\hbox{log}^{2}(n)\}
. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
,
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
. Then there exists constants
c_{2},c_{3},c_{4},c_{5},c_{6},c_{7},c_{8},c_{9},c_{10}>0

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-c_{2}n\min(c_{3}t^{2},c_{4}t)+2(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)\right),

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-c_{7}n\min(c_{5}t^{2},c_{6}t)+(m+k)\hbox{log}(9)+\hbox{log}(p)\right),

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|>nt\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(-c_{10}n\min(c_{8}t^{2},c_{9}t)+2\hbox{log}(p)\right).

Proof: The lemma is a consequence of Lemma 15 in
Loh & Wainwright 2012
and using the same arguments as those
lead to Lemma
B.7
.∎

Corollary
B.5
.

Assume that Conditions
(C1)
holds. If

{\bf X}_{i},{\bf U}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
,
{\mathbb{K}}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1\}
,
{\mathbb{K}}_{1}\equiv\{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{(\mathcal{M}\cup S)^{c}}:\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=1,\|{\bf v}\|_{0}=1\}
. There are constants
a_{4}>0,a_{5}>0,b_{5}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|\right.

\displaystyle\left.>na_{4}\sqrt{\frac{(m+k)}{n}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left\{-(m+k)\right\},

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|\right.

\displaystyle\left.>na_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\max[(m+k),\hbox{log}(p)]}{n}}\right)\leq 2\exp[-\max\{\hbox{log}(p),(m+k)\}],

and

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sup_{{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1},{\bf w}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{1}}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w}-E({\bf v}^{\rm T}({\bf W}_{i}-\bm{\Omega}\bm{\beta})^{\otimes 2}{\bf w})\}|\right.

\displaystyle\left.>nb_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right)\leq 2\exp\{-\hbox{log}(p)\}.

Proof: The corollary follows the same arguments as those
lead to Corollary
B.2
.∎

B.3
Lemmas on Criterion Function and Penalty Function

Lemma
B.10
.

Consider a
\mu
-amenable regularizer
\rho_{\lambda}
, with
\mu<\alpha_{1}
and
n>\hbox{log}(p)\tau_{1}^{2}(m+k)^{2}/(\alpha_{1}-\mu)^{2}
,
where
\alpha_{1},\tau_{1}
are defined in Lemma
B.6
. Then the function
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2
and
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})+\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})
are strictly convex on

\bm{\beta}\in\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
, and hence the restricted program
(
14
) is also strictly convex.

Proof:
First define a vector
{\bf v}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
with the
j
th element

|v_{j}|>0
if
j\in\mathcal{M}\cup S
, and
\|v_{j}\|=0
, otherwise. By Lemma
B.6
, we have for
\bm{\beta}
in the feasible set of
program (
14
),

\displaystyle{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}\geq\alpha_{1}\|{\bf v}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\|{\bf v}\|_{1}^{2},

and
\|{\bf v}\|_{1}\leq\sqrt{m+k}\|{\bf v}\|_{2}
, and hence we
have

\displaystyle{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}\geq\left\{\alpha_{1}-\tau_{1}(m+k)\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right\}\|{\bf v}\|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle{\bf v}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}^{\rm T}\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\right\}_{(\mathcal{M}+S)(\mathcal{M}+S)}{\bf v}_{\mathcal{M}\cup S}-\mu{\bf v}_{S}^{\rm T}{\bf v}_{S}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\{\alpha_{1}-\mu-\tau_{1}(m+k)\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\right\}\|{\bf v}\|_{2}^{2},

where
\left\{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})/(\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T})\right\}_{(\mathcal{M}\cup S),(\mathcal{M}\cup S)}
is the
(m+k)\times(m+k)
block of
\left\{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})/\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\right\}
corresponding to
\mathcal{M}\cup S
.
Hence
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2
is strictly convex on

\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
.

Finally, because

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})=(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2)+\{\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\},

where the second part is convex over
\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
by Lemma
B.4
,
hence
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})
restricted to

\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
is strictly
convex. Because
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})+\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})=\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
, the strict convexity of
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})+\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})
over
\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{M}\cup S}
follows. This proves the
result. ∎

Now as we know
{\bf C}
contains
r
independent columns, without loss of
generality, we write
{\bf C}=({\bf C}_{r},{\bf C}_{m-r})
where
{\bf C}_{r}
is a full
rank square matrix.

Lemma
B.11
.

Let
{\bf A},{\bf B}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
be invertible. For any matrix
norm
\|\|
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}^{-1}-{\bf B}^{-1}\|\leq\frac{\|{\bf A}^{-1}\|^{2}\|{\bf A}-{\bf B}\|}{1-\|{\bf A}^{-1}\|\|{\bf A}-{\bf B}\|}.

In particular, if
\|{\bf A}^{-1}\|\|{\bf A}-{\bf B}\|\leq 1/2
, then
\|{\bf A}^{-1}-{\bf B}^{-1}\|=O(\|{\bf A}^{-1}\|^{2}\|{\bf A}-{\bf B}\|)
.

Proof: This lemma is Lemma 11 in

Loh & Wainwright 2017
. ∎

Lemma
B.12
.

Suppose
{\bf x}^{*}
is feasible for the program

\displaystyle\min_{{\bf x}}\{f({\bf x})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}\},\text{ such that }\|{\bf x}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|{\bf x}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},\text{ and }{\bf C}{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}}={\bf t},

(B.1)

where
f\in C^{2}
,
g\in C^{1}
and
g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\kappa/2\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}
is concave and
{\bf C}
is an
r\times m

matrix. Define
{\bf A}=\{{\bf 0}_{(p-m)\times m},{\bf I}_{(p-m)\times(p-m)}\}
and

{\bf A}_{1}=\{{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times(p-m)}\}
. Assume there are

{\bf v}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
,
{\bf w}_{1}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}
,
{\bf w}_{2}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
,

\mu_{1}^{*}\geq 0
,
\mu_{2}^{*}\geq 0
,
\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}

such that

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{*}(R_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1})=0

(B.2)

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{*}(R_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2})=0

(B.3)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}+\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}=0

(B.4)

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}f({\bf x})}{\partial{\bf x}\partial{\bf x}^{\rm T}}{\bf s}>\kappa,\forall{\bf s}\in G^{*},

(B.5)

where

\displaystyle G^{*}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\{{\bf s}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}:\|{\bf s}\|_{2}=1;\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}\leq 0\text{ if }\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}=R_{1};\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}\leq 0\text{ if }\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}=R_{2};\right.

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}\left(\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}}\right)+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}={0};

\displaystyle\left.\mu_{1}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}=0,\mu_{2}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}=0;{\bf C}{\bf s}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bf 0\right\}.

Then
{\bf x}^{*}
is an isolated local minimum of the program (
B.1
).

Proof: The proof of this lemma is similar to the proof
of Theorem 3 in
Fletcher & Watson 1980
and that of Lemma 10 in
Loh & Wainwright 2017
,
except that we allow additional constraints
\|{\bf x}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}
and

{\bf C}{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}}={\bf t}
.

Suppose
{\bf x}^{*}
is not an isolated local minimum. Then there is a
sequence
({\bf x}^{k})
, so that
{\bf x}^{k}\to{\bf x}^{*}
and

\displaystyle\phi({\bf x}^{k})\leq\phi({\bf x}^{*}),

where
\phi({\bf x})=f({\bf x})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
. Let
{\bf s}^{k}:=({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
, so

({\bf s}^{k})
is a set of feasible directions. Since
({\bf s}^{k})\subset\mathbb{B}_{2}(1)
, where
\mathbb{B}_{2}(1)
is the ball with radius
1, the set must possess a point of accumulation
{\bf s}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(1)
, and we can extract a convergence subsequence such
that
({\bf s}^{k})\to{\bf s}
. With a slight abuse of notation, we still use
({\bf s}^{k})
to denote
the subsequence. We will show that
{\bf s}\in G^{*}
.

First of all by the construction,
{\bf x}^{k}
’s are all feasible, and hence

{\bf C}{\bf x}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}}={\bf t}
. Therefore,
{\bf C}{\bf s}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}}=0
, take the limits on
the left and right of the equation implies

\displaystyle{\bf C}{\bf s}_{\mathcal{M}}=\bf 0.

(B.6)

Further, because the feasible region is closed,
{\bf s}
is also in the
feasible direction at
{\bf x}^{*}
. If
\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}=R_{1}
, by the
sub-gradient of convexity function
\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}
we have

\displaystyle 0\geq\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}=\|{\bf x}^{*}+\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{\bf s}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}\geq\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{{\bf s}^{k}}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1},

for any
{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}
. When
k\to\infty
,
this leads to

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}\leq 0.

(B.7)

Further (
B.2
) also implies that if
\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}\neq R_{1}
, then

\mu_{1}^{*}=0
. Since
\mu_{1}^{*}\geq 0
, hence we have

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}\leq 0.

(B.8)

Similarly, by the
sub-gradient of convexity function
\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
we have

\displaystyle 0\geq\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}=\|{\bf x}^{*}+\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{\bf s}^{k}\|_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}\geq\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{{\bf s}^{k}}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2},

for any
{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
. When
k\to\infty
,
this leads to

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}\leq 0.

(B.9)

Further (
B.3
) also implies that if
\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}\neq R_{2}
, then

\mu_{2}^{*}=0
. Since
\mu_{2}^{*}\geq 0
, hence we have

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}\leq 0.

(B.10)

Further by (
B.4
), we have

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+{\bf s}^{\rm T}\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}+\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}+{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*^{\rm T}}=0

which, together with (
B.6
), implies

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+{\bf s}^{\rm T}\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}=-\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}-\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}\geq 0.

(B.11)

By the definition of sub-gradient, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}+\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{\bf A}{\bf s}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}\geq\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{\bf s}^{k\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}

for all
{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}
and all
k
. Further
because
\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}+\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}{\bf A}{\bf s}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}=\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}=\lim_{k\to\infty}{\bf s}^{k\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}\leq\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}}{\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}

(B.12)

for all
{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}
.
Furthermore,

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}

(B.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}{\bf s}^{k\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{k\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{\langle{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*},\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\rangle}{\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{f({\bf x}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})-f({\bf x}^{*})+g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})}{\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}},

since
{\bf x}^{k}\to{\bf x}^{*}
and
f({\bf x}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})\in C^{1}
. Combining (
B.12
) and (
B.13
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{\phi({\bf x}^{k})-\phi({\bf x}^{*})}{\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}*\|}\leq 0.

Combining with (
B.11
), we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 0,

hence

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}=0

(B.14)

Now together with (
B.4
), we have

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}+\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}=0.

Further by (
B.8
) and (
B.10
), we have

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}=\mu_{2}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}=0.

(B.15)

Combine (
B.6
), (
B.7
), (
B.9
), (
B.14
)
and (
B.15
), we conclude that
{\bf s}\in G^{*}
.

By the convexity of the
L_{1}
norm, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}=\|{\bf x}^{*}+({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})\|_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}\geq({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}={\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}

for all
{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}
. Therefore,
{\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}\leq\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{1}\leq R_{1}
. Similarly,
by the convexity of the
L_{2}
norm, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}=\|{\bf x}^{*}+({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})\|_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}\geq({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}={\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}

for all
{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
. Therefore,
{\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}\leq\|{\bf x}^{k}\|_{2}\leq R_{2}
.
Further,
{\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}-{\bf t}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}=0
.
Hence

\displaystyle\phi({\bf x}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f({\bf x}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})+\lambda\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle f({\bf x}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})+\lambda{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}({\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}-R_{1})+\mu_{2}^{*}({\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}-R_{2})

\displaystyle+{\bf x}^{k\rm T}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}-{\bf t}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}

for all
{\bf v}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}\|_{1}
,
and

\displaystyle\phi({\bf x}^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f({\bf x}^{*})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})+\lambda{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}({\bf x}^{*\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}-R_{1})+\mu_{2}^{*}({\bf x}^{*\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}-R_{2})

\displaystyle+{\bf x}^{*\rm T}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}-{\bf t}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*}.

The equality holds because
\mu_{1}^{*}({\bf x}^{*\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}-R_{1})=0

and
\mu_{2}^{*}({\bf x}^{*\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}-R_{2})=0
.
Hence

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\{\phi({\bf x}^{k})-\phi({\bf x}^{*})\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

(B.16)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\{f({\bf x}^{k})-f({\bf x}^{*})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})+g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})+\langle\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}+\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}

\displaystyle+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{3}^{*},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\rangle\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\{f({\bf x}^{k})-f({\bf x}^{*})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})+g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\right\rangle/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\left\{f({\bf x}^{k})-f({\bf x}^{*})-\left\langle\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\right\rangle\right\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left\{g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})-\left\langle{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\right\rangle\right\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

By the concavity of
g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\kappa/2\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|^{2}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\{g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{k})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})-\left\langle{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\right\rangle\right\}\leq\kappa/2\|{\bf x}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}.

Further note that
\phi({\bf x}^{k})-\phi({\bf x}^{*})\leq 0
. Combine with
(
B.16
), we have

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\left\{f({\bf x}^{k})-f({\bf x}^{*})-\left\langle\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}},{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\right\rangle\right\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\kappa/2\|{\bf x}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 0,

which by Taylor expansion implies

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}\partial{\bf x}^{\rm T}}{\bf s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}\partial{\bf x}^{\rm T}}({\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*})/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\{\kappa\|{\bf x}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}+o(\|({\bf x}^{k}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\|_{2}^{2})\}/\|{\bf x}^{k}-{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa,

which contradicts with (
B.5
). Hence
{\bf x}^{*}
must be an isolated
local minimum. ∎

Corollary
B.6
.

Suppose
{\bf x}^{*}
is feasible for the program

\displaystyle\min_{{\bf x}}\{f({\bf x})-g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})+\lambda\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}\},\text{ such that }\|{\bf x}\|_{1}\leq R_{1},\|{\bf x}\|_{2}\leq R_{2},

(B.17)

where
f\in C^{2}
,
g\in C^{1}
and
g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})-\kappa/2\|{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}
is concave and
{\bf C}
is an
r\times m

matrix. Define
{\bf A}=\{{\bf 0}_{(p-m)\times m},{\bf I}_{(p-m)\times(p-m)}\}
and

{\bf A}_{1}=\{{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times(p-m)}\}
. Assume there are

{\bf v}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
,
{\bf w}_{1}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}
,
{\bf w}_{2}^{*}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}
,
\mu_{1}^{*}\geq 0
,
\mu_{2}^{*}\geq 0

such that

\displaystyle\mu_{1}^{*}(R_{1}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1})=0

\displaystyle\mu_{2}^{*}(R_{2}-\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2})=0

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}+\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}^{*}+\mu_{1}^{*}{\bf w}_{1}^{*}+\mu_{2}^{*}{\bf w}_{2}^{*}=0

\displaystyle{\bf s}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}f({\bf x})}{\partial{\bf x}\partial{\bf x}^{\rm T}}{\bf s}>\kappa,\forall{\bf s}\in G^{*},

where

\displaystyle G^{*}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\{{\bf s}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}:\|{\bf s}\|_{2}=1;\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}\leq 0\text{ if }\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}=R_{1};\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}\leq 0\text{ if }\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}=R_{2};\right.

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf v}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}\left(\frac{\partial f({\bf x}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}^{*}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial g({\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*})}{\partial{\bf x}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{*}}\right)+\lambda{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf v}={0};

\displaystyle\left.\mu_{1}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{1}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{1}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{1}=0,\mu_{2}^{*}\sup_{{\bf w}_{2}\in\partial\|{\bf x}^{*}\|_{2}}{\bf s}^{\rm T}{\bf w}_{2}=0\right\}.

Then
{\bf x}^{*}
is an isolated local minimum of the program (
B.17
).

Proof: The Corollary holds by using the same argument as
those lead to Lemma
B.12
, while ignoring the equality
constraint.∎

Lemma
B.13
.

Assume Conditions
(C1)
–
(C6)
hold,
\lambda\leq\alpha_{1}/(8R_{1})
,
R_{1}\leq[n\alpha_{1}^{2}/\{64\tau_{1}^{2}\hbox{log}(p)\}]^{1/4}
,
\delta\in[4R_{1}\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}/\lambda,1]
,
n\geq 4\hbox{log}(p)\tau_{1}^{2}\{\sqrt{r}\|{\bf C}_{r}^{-1}{\bf C}_{m-r}\|_{2}+\sqrt{m-r}+(2/\delta+1/2)\sqrt{k}\}^{4}/(\alpha_{1}-\mu)^{2}
, and

\alpha_{1}>\mu
. Define
{\bf A}=\{{\bf 0}_{(p-m)\times m},{\bf I}_{(p-m)\times(p-m)}\}
and

{\bf A}_{1}=\{{\bf I}_{m\times m},{\bf 0}_{m\times(p-m)}\}
. Suppose

\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
is a stationary point of program (
7
) and

\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is the interior local minimizer of (
7
) and satisfies

\hbox{supp}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\subseteq\mathcal{M}\cup S
. Then
\hbox{supp}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})\subseteq\mathcal{M}\cup S
.

Proof: Let
\widetilde{{\bf v}}:=\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
, by the Taylor
expansion of the first order derivative

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}=\widetilde{\bf v}^{\rm T}\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta}^{*})/\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widetilde{\bf v},

where
\bm{\beta}^{*}
is a point on the line connecting
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
and

\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
and hence in the feasible set. Therefore, by Lemma

B.6
we have

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{1}^{2}.

We first show
that
\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq 1
. Suppose that
\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{2}>1
, we have

\displaystyle\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}-2\tau_{1}R_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{1}.

(B.18)

The first order condition implies

\displaystyle\left\{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}+{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\right\}^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})\geq 0

(B.19)

and hence

\displaystyle\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}\widetilde{{\bf v}}\leq-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}\widetilde{{\bf v}}.

Combine with (
B.18
), we have

\displaystyle\{-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}\}^{\rm T}\widetilde{{\bf v}}\geq\alpha_{1}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}-2\tau_{1}R_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{1}.

(B.20)

Further because
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is an interior local minimum,
we have

\displaystyle\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}+{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}=\bf 0.

for some Lagrange multiplier
\bm{\mu}_{4}>{\bf 0}
.
Note that
({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4})^{\rm T}\widetilde{\bf v}=\bm{\mu}_{t}^{\rm T}{\bf C}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{mM})={\bf 0}
.
Therefore, plug in (
B.20
),
we have

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}-2\tau_{1}R_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\}^{\rm T}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\}^{\rm T}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\{\|{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{\infty}+\|{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{\infty}\}\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\lambda\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{1},

where
\|{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{\infty}\leq\lambda
holds by Lemma
B.4
. Hence we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{2}\leq\{2\lambda+2\tau_{1}R_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\}\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{1}/\alpha_{1}\leq 2R_{1}\{2\lambda+2\tau_{1}R_{1}\sqrt{\hbox{log}(p)/n}\}/\alpha_{1}.

The right hand side is at most 1 because
\lambda\leq\alpha_{1}/(8R_{1})
,
and
n\geq\hbox{log}(p)64\tau_{1}^{2}R_{1}^{4}/\alpha_{1}^{2}
, which
contradicts with
\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}>1
. Therefore
\|\widetilde{\bf v}\|_{2}\leq 1
.

Now note that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})=(\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2)+\{\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\}

and
\{\mu\|\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{2}^{2}/2-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\}
is convex by Lemma

B.4
, and hence for any
\bm{\beta}
in the feasible
set, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bf v}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}\{\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})-q_{\lambda}(\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})\}}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bf v}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\beta})}{\partial\bm{\beta}\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\mu\widetilde{{\bf v}}^{\rm T}{\bf A}^{\rm T}{\bf A}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

(B.21)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\alpha_{1}-\mu)\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}-\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{1}^{2}.

Further by (
B.19
), we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})+\lambda\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}),

where
\widetilde{{\bf z}}\in\partial\|\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
. Further, because
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}

is an interior local minimum, for
\widehat{{\bf z}}\in\partial\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})/\partial\bm{\beta}+{\bf A}^{\rm T}\partial\rho_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})/\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}}-{\bf A}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}}\right\}+\lambda{\bf A}^{\rm T}\widehat{{\bf z}}+{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\bm{\mu}_{4},

which leads to

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}+\lambda(\widehat{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widetilde{{\bf v}}=\bf 0.

Hence

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle+\lambda(\widehat{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-\lambda(\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle+\lambda(\widehat{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}-\lambda\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}-\lambda\|\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}+\lambda(\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widehat{\bm{\beta}},

which implies

\displaystyle\lambda\|\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}-\lambda(\widehat{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}

(B.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\lambda\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}+\lambda(\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widehat{\bm{\beta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}-\left\{\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial q_{\lambda}(\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}})}{\partial\bm{\beta}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}^{\rm T}}{\bf A}\right\}\widetilde{{\bf v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\hbox{log}(p)}{n}}\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{1}^{2}-(\alpha_{1}-\mu)\|\widetilde{{\bf v}}\|_{2}^{2}.

The second inequality holds because
|(\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}{\bf A})\widehat{\bm{\beta}}|=|\widetilde{{\bf z}}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}|\leq\|\widetilde{{\bf z}}\|_{\infty}\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\mathcal{M}^{c}}\|_{1}
, and
